      (function () {
        function at(n) {
          n = Math.trunc(n) || 0;
          if (n < 0) n += this.length;
          if (n < 0 || n >= this.length) return undefined;
          return this[n];
        }
        [Array, String].forEach(function (C) {
          if (!C.prototype.at) {
            Object.defineProperty(C.prototype, "at", { value: at, writable: true, configurable: true });
          }
        });
      })();
    </script>

  <section class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm px-6 py-5 mt-4">
    <h2 class="text-lg font-black text-gray-900 mb-3 font-display">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
    <div class="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">What is an NFL Big Board?</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed mt-1">An NFL Big Board is a pre-draft ranking of the top prospects eligible for the upcoming NFL Draft, ordered by overall talent rather than by team need. Analysts publish their own big boards; a consensus big board aggregates many of them into one ranking.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">How is the 2027 NFL Consensus Big Board calculated?</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed mt-1">Every tracked source contributes to a weighted score for each prospect. Newer rankings carry more weight than older ones, and the highest aggregate score ranks #1.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">How often is the 2027 Consensus Big Board updated?</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed mt-1">Daily. New analyst big boards, mock drafts, and team-by-team mocks are scraped overnight and folded into the consensus first thing each morning.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Who publishes the rankings that feed the consensus?</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed mt-1">Major outlets like ESPN, NFL.com, CBS, PFF, The Athletic, Bleacher Report, and Yahoo Sports, plus dozens of independent analysts and team-specific writers tracked across the web.</p>
        </div>
    </div>
  </section>
